\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df\_res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}rbind\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, df\_res)}
 
\NormalTok{df\_res\_tot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_res }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(channel\_from, channel\_to) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(n)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(channel\_from) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{tot\_n =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(n),}
               \AttributeTok{perc =}\NormalTok{ n }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_n) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `summarise()` has grouped output by 'channel_from'. You can override using the `.groups` argument.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df\_dummy }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{channel\_from =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(start)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(conversion)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(null)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                       \AttributeTok{channel\_to =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(start)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(conversion)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(null)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                       \AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
                       \AttributeTok{tot\_n =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
                       \AttributeTok{perc =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
 
\NormalTok{df\_res\_tot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot, df\_dummy)}
 
\CommentTok{\# comparing transition matrices}
\NormalTok{trans\_matrix\_prob\_m }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot, channel\_from }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ channel\_to, }\AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}perc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =}\NormalTok{ sum)}
\NormalTok{trans\_matrix\_prob }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(mod\_attrib\_alt}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{transition\_matrix)}
\NormalTok{trans\_matrix\_prob }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(trans\_matrix\_prob, channel\_from }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ channel\_to, }\AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}transition\_probability\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
 
\CommentTok{\# computing attribution {-} \textquotesingle{}manual\textquotesingle{} way}
\NormalTok{channels\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_path\_1\_clean\_multi }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(conversion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(channel)}
\NormalTok{channels\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(channels\_list}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{channel)}
 
\NormalTok{df\_res\_ini }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_res\_tot }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(channel\_from, channel\_to)}
\NormalTok{df\_attrib }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{vector}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}list\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(channels\_list))}
 
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(channels\_list))) \{}
         
\NormalTok{        channel }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ channels\_list[i]}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_res1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_res }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{channel\_from =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(channel\_from }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ channel, }\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, channel\_from),}
                       \AttributeTok{channel\_to =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(channel\_to }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ channel, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(null)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, channel\_to)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{()}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_res\_tot1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_res1 }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(channel\_from, channel\_to) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(n)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                 
                \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(channel\_from) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{tot\_n =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(n),}
                       \AttributeTok{perc =}\NormalTok{ n }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_n) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
                \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_res\_tot1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot1, df\_dummy) }\CommentTok{\# adding (start), (conversion) and (null) states}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_res\_tot1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_ini, df\_res\_tot1, }\AttributeTok{by =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_from\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_to\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{        df\_res\_tot1[}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot1)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_trans1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot1, channel\_from }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ channel\_to, }\AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}perc\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =}\NormalTok{ sum)}
         
\NormalTok{        trans\_matrix\_1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_trans1}
        \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(trans\_matrix\_1) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ trans\_matrix\_1}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{channel\_from}
\NormalTok{        trans\_matrix\_1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.matrix}\NormalTok{(trans\_matrix\_1[, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{])}
         
\NormalTok{        inist\_n1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df\_res\_tot1, channel\_from }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ channel\_to, }\AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}n\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =}\NormalTok{ sum)}
        \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(inist\_n1) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ inist\_n1}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{channel\_from}
\NormalTok{        inist\_n1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.matrix}\NormalTok{(inist\_n1[, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{])}
\NormalTok{        inist\_n1[}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(inist\_n1)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{        inist\_n1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ inist\_n1[}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(start)\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, ]}
         
\NormalTok{        res\_num1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ inist\_n1 }\SpecialCharTok{\%*\%}\NormalTok{ (trans\_matrix\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\%\^{}\%} \DecValTok{100000}\NormalTok{)}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{channel\_name =}\NormalTok{ channel,}
                               \AttributeTok{conversions =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(res\_num1[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]))}
         
\NormalTok{        df\_attrib[[i]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_cache}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df\_attrib }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}rbind\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, df\_attrib)}
 
\CommentTok{\# computing removal effect and results}
\NormalTok{tot\_conv }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(df\_multi\_paths\_m}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conversion)}
 
\NormalTok{df\_attrib }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_attrib }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{tot\_conversions =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(df\_multi\_paths\_m}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conversion),}
               \AttributeTok{impact =}\NormalTok{ (tot\_conversions }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ conversions) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_conversions,}
               \AttributeTok{tot\_impact =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(impact),}
               \AttributeTok{weighted\_impact =}\NormalTok{ impact }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot\_impact,}
               \AttributeTok{attrib\_model\_conversions =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(tot\_conversions }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ weighted\_impact)}
\NormalTok{        ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(channel\_name, attrib\_model\_conversions)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Since with different transition matrices, the removal effects and attribution results stay the same, in practice we skip duplicates.

\hypertarget{non-conversion-paths}{%
\paragraph{5. Non-conversion paths}\label{non-conversion-paths}}

We incorporate null paths in this analysis.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\# Generic Probabilistic Model \#\#\#\#\#}
\NormalTok{df\_all\_paths\_compl }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_path\_1\_clean }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(customer\_id) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{path =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(channel, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{} \textgreater{} \textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                  \AttributeTok{conversion =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(conversion)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{null\_conversion =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(conversion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
 
\NormalTok{mod\_attrib\_complete }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{markov\_model}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df\_all\_paths\_compl,}
        \AttributeTok{var\_path =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}path\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{var\_conv =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}conversion\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{var\_null =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}null\_conversion\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{out\_more =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\includegraphics{11-empirical_model_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-22-2.pdf}

In conclusion, we say that if a customer made contact with a marketing channel the first time for more than 23 days and/or hasn't made contact with a marketing channel for the last 12 days, then it is a fruitless path.

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# extracting data for generic model}
\NormalTok{df\_multi\_paths\_tl\_3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_path\_1\_clean\_multi }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(customer\_id) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{prev\_time\_lapse =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(date }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{lag}\NormalTok{(date)))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{path =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(channel, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{} \textgreater{} \textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                  \AttributeTok{tot\_time\_lapse =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(date) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(date))),}
                  \AttributeTok{prev\_touch\_tl =}\NormalTok{ prev\_time\_lapse[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(date) }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ date)],}
                  \AttributeTok{conversion =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(conversion)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{is\_fruitless =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(conversion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ tot\_time\_lapse }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{20} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ prev\_touch\_tl }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(conversion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ is\_fruitless }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{channel-comparisons}{%
\paragraph{7. Channel Comparisons}\label{channel-comparisons}}

We can use \texttt{markov\_model} with \texttt{var\_value} to compare the gross margin among channels.

\hypertarget{example-3}{%
\subsubsection{Example 3}\label{example-3}}

This example is from \href{https://www.bounteous.com/insights/2016/06/30/marketing-channel-attribution-markov-models-r/}{Bounteus}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Install these libraries (only do this once)}
\CommentTok{\# install.packages("ChannelAttribution")}
\CommentTok{\# install.packages("reshape")}
\CommentTok{\# install.packages("ggplot2")}

\CommentTok{\# Load these libraries (every time you start RStudio)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ChannelAttribution)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggplot2)}

\CommentTok{\# This loads the demo data. You can load your own data by importing a dataset or reading in a file}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(PathData)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Path Variable -- The steps a user takes across sessions to comprise the sequences.
\item
  Conversion Variable -- How many times a user converted.
\item
  Value Variable -- The monetary value of each marketing channel.
\item
  Null Variable -- How many times a user exited.
\end{itemize}

Build the simple heuristic models (First Click / first\_touch, Last Click / last\_touch, and Linear Attribution / linear\_touch):

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{H }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{heuristic\_models}\NormalTok{(Data, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}path\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}total\_conversions\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{var\_value=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}total\_conversion\_value\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Markov model

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{M }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{markov\_model}\NormalTok{(Data, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}path\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}total\_conversions\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{var\_value=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}total\_conversion\_value\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{order =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Number of simulations: 100000 - Convergence reached: 1.46% < 5.00%
## 
## Percentage of simulated paths that successfully end before maximum number of steps (46) is reached: 99.99%
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Merges the two data frames on the "channel\_name" column.}
\NormalTok{R }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(H, M, }\AttributeTok{by=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_name\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }

\CommentTok{\# Selects only relevant columns}
\NormalTok{R1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ R[, (}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(R)}\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_name\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}first\_touch\_conversions\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}last\_touch\_conversions\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}linear\_touch\_conversions\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}total\_conversion\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))]}

\CommentTok{\# Renames the columns}
\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(R1) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_name\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}first\_touch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}last\_touch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}linear\_touch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}markov\_model\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }

\CommentTok{\# Transforms the dataset into a data frame that ggplot2 can use to graph the outcomes}
\NormalTok{R1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{melt}\NormalTok{(R1, }\AttributeTok{id=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_name\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Plot the total conversions

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(R1, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(channel\_name, value, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ variable)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}identity\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position=}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}dodge\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{ggtitle}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}TOTAL CONVERSIONS\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{axis.title.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vjust =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{axis.title.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vjust =} \SpecialCharTok{+}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{16}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{plot.title=}\FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{ylab}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\includegraphics{11-empirical_model_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-28-1.pdf}

The ``Total Conversions'' bar chart shows you how many conversions were attributed to each channel (i.e.~alpha, beta, etc.) for each method (i.e.~first\_touch, last\_touch, etc.).

\hypertarget{sales-funnel}{%
\section{Sales Funnel}\label{sales-funnel}}

\hypertarget{example-1-1}{%
\subsection{Example 1}\label{example-1-1}}

This example is based on \href{https://www.analyzecore.com/2017/09/28/marketing-multi-channel-attribution-model-based-on-sales-funnel-with-r/}{Sergey Bryl}

\[
Awareness \to Interest \to Desire \to Action 
\]

Step in the funnel:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  0 step (necessary condition) -- customer visits a site for the first time
\item
  1st step (awareness) -- visits two site's pages
\item
  2nd step (interest) -- reviews a product page
\item
  3rd step (desire) -- adds a product to the shopping cart
\item
  4th step (action) -- completes purchase
\end{itemize}

Simulate data

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(purrrlyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}
 
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\# simulating the "real" data \#\#\#\#\#}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{454}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df\_raw }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{customer\_id =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}id\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{date =} \FunctionTok{as.POSIXct}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{rbeta}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10000}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{10000000}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{origin =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2017{-}01{-}01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{tz =} \StringTok{"UTC"}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{channel =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\_\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{7}\NormalTok{),}
      \DecValTok{10000}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.12}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.03}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.07}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.08}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )),}
    \AttributeTok{site\_visit =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{two\_pages\_visit =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{10000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.8}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{product\_page\_visit =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      two\_pages\_visit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(two\_pages\_visit[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(two\_pages\_visit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)]),}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.75}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      ),}
      \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{add\_to\_cart =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      product\_page\_visit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(product\_page\_visit[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(product\_page\_visit }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)]),}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      ),}
      \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{purchase =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(add\_to\_cart }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
                      \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
                        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
                        \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(add\_to\_cart[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(add\_to\_cart }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)]),}
                        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
                        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.02}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.98}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                      ),}
                      \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{dmap\_at}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}customer\_id\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{), as.character) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(date) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{session\_id =} \FunctionTok{row\_number}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(customer\_id, session\_id)}
\NormalTok{df\_raw }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
\NormalTok{  reshape2}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{melt}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    df\_raw,}
    \AttributeTok{id.vars =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}customer\_id\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}date\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}channel\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}session\_id\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{value.name =} \StringTok{"trigger"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{variable.name =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}event\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(trigger }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{trigger) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(customer\_id, date)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Preprocessing

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# removing not first events }\AlertTok{\#\#\#}
\NormalTok{df\_customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_raw }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(customer\_id, event) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(date }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(date)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Assumption: all customers are first-time buyers. Hence, every next purchase as an event will be removed with the above code.

Calculate channel probability

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Sales Funnel probabilities }\AlertTok{\#\#\#}
\NormalTok{sf\_probs }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_customers }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(event) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{customers\_on\_step =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{sf\_probs =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(customers\_on\_step }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ customers\_on\_step[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{sf\_probs\_step =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(customers\_on\_step }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{lag}\NormalTok{(customers\_on\_step), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{sf\_probs\_step =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(sf\_probs\_step) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, sf\_probs\_step),}
        \AttributeTok{sf\_importance =} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ sf\_probs\_step,}
        \AttributeTok{sf\_importance\_weighted =}\NormalTok{ sf\_importance }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(sf\_importance)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Visualization

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# Sales Funnel visualization }\AlertTok{\#\#\#}
\NormalTok{df\_customers\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_customers }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(event) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(channel) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{pl =} \FunctionTok{row\_number}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{pl\_new =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}two\_pages\_visit\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}two\_pages\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
\NormalTok{            event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\_page\_visit\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\_page\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
\NormalTok{            event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}add\_to\_cart\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}add\_to\_cart\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
\NormalTok{            event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}purchase\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(pl[event }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}purchase\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
            \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{pl =}\NormalTok{ pl }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ pl\_new}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{df\_customers\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{event }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df\_customers\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{event,}
        \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}purchase\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}add\_to\_cart\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\_page\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}two\_pages\_visit\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}site\_visit\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    )}

\CommentTok{\# color palette}
\NormalTok{cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\#4e79a7\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\#f28e2b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}

    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\#9c755f\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\#bab0ac\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(df\_customers\_plot, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ event, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ pl)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{scale\_colour\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =}\NormalTok{ cols) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ customer\_id, }\AttributeTok{color =} \FunctionTok{as.factor}\NormalTok{(channel)), }\AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ sf\_probs,}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ event,}
            \AttributeTok{y =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(sf\_probs }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{fontface =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}bold\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{guides}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{color =} \FunctionTok{guide\_legend}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{override.aes =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}bottom\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{legend.direction =} \StringTok{"horizontal"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{panel.grid.major.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{panel.grid.minor =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{plot.title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{vjust =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}black\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{lineheight =} \FloatTok{0.8}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.title.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{16}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.title.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{hjust =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{vjust =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"plain"}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{ggtitle}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Sales Funnel visualization {-} all customers journeys"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\includegraphics{11-empirical_model_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-33-1.pdf}

Calculate attribution

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\# computing attribution }\AlertTok{\#\#\#}
\NormalTok{df\_attrib }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_customers }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# removing customers without purchase}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(customer\_id) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{any}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(event) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}purchase\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \CommentTok{\# joining step\textquotesingle{}s importances}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(., sf\_probs }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(event, sf\_importance\_weighted), }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}event\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(channel) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{tot\_attribution =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(sf\_importance\_weighted)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{example-2-1}{%
\subsection{Example 2}\label{example-2-1}}

Code from \href{https://www.analyzecore.com/2015/06/23/sales-funnel-visualization-with-r/}{Sergey Bryl}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggplot2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}

\CommentTok{\# creating a data samples}
\CommentTok{\# content}
\NormalTok{df.content }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{content =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}main\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ad landing\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product 1\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product 2\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product 3\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product 4\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}shopping cart\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}thank you page\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{step =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}awareness\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}awareness\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}interest\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}interest\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}interest\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}interest\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desire\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}action\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{number =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{150000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{80000}\NormalTok{,}
               \DecValTok{80000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{40000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{25000}\NormalTok{,}
               \DecValTok{130000}\NormalTok{,}
               \DecValTok{120000}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\# customers}
\NormalTok{df.customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{content =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}new\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}engaged\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}loyal\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{step =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}new\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}engaged\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}loyal\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{number =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{25000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{40000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{55000}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\# combining two data sets}
\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df.content, df.customers)}

\CommentTok{\# calculating dummies, max and min values of X for plotting}
\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(step) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{totnum =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(number)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{dum =}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(totnum) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ totnum) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
           \AttributeTok{maxx =}\NormalTok{ totnum }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ dum,}
           \AttributeTok{minx =}\NormalTok{ dum)}

\CommentTok{\# data frame for plotting funnel lines}
\NormalTok{df.lines }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(step, maxx, minx)}

\CommentTok{\# data frame with dummies}
\NormalTok{df.dum }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(step, dum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{content =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}dummy\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
           \AttributeTok{number =}\NormalTok{ dum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(content, step, number)}

\CommentTok{\# data frame with rates}
\NormalTok{conv }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{totnum[df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{step }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}action\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{]}

\NormalTok{df.rates }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(step, totnum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{prevnum =} \FunctionTok{lag}\NormalTok{(totnum),}
        \AttributeTok{rate =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            step }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}new\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ step }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}engaged\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ step }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}loyal\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(totnum }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ conv, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
            \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(totnum }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ prevnum, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(step, rate)}
\NormalTok{df.rates }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(df.rates)}

\CommentTok{\# creting final data frame}
\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(content, step, number)}

\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df.all, df.dum)}

\CommentTok{\# defining order of steps}
\NormalTok{df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{step }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{step,}
        \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}loyal\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}engaged\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}new\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}action\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}desire\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}interest\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}awareness\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(step))}
\NormalTok{list1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(content) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(content }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}dummy\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{content }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{content, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(list1}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{content), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}dummy\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# calculating position of labels}
\NormalTok{df.all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.all }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(step, }\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(content)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(step) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{pos =} \FunctionTok{cumsum}\NormalTok{(number) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FloatTok{0.5} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ number) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}

\CommentTok{\# creating custom palette with \textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{} color for dummies}
\NormalTok{cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"\#fec44f"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#a1d99b"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fee0d2"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#2ca25f"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#8856a7"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#43a2ca"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fdbb84"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#e34a33"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#a6bddb"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#dd1c77"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#ffffff"}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# plotting chart}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{coord\_flip}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =}\NormalTok{ cols) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.all,}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ number, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ content),}
        \AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.all[df.all}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{content }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}dummy\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,],}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step,}
            \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ pos,}
            \AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(content, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}{-}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, number }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}K\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{fontface =} \StringTok{"bold"}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_ribbon}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.lines,}
                \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
                    \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step,}
                    \AttributeTok{ymax =} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(maxx),}
                    \AttributeTok{ymin =}\NormalTok{ maxx,}
                    \AttributeTok{group =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{                ),}
                \AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.lines,}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ maxx, }\AttributeTok{group =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}darkred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_ribbon}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.lines,}
                \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
                    \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step,}
                    \AttributeTok{ymax =}\NormalTok{ minx,}
                    \AttributeTok{ymin =} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(minx),}
                    \AttributeTok{group =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{                ),}
                \AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.lines,}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ minx, }\AttributeTok{group =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}darkred\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df.rates,}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ step,}
            \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ (df.lines}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{minx[}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]),}
            \AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(rate }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{hjust =} \FloatTok{1.2}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}darkblue\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{fontface =} \StringTok{"bold"}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}none\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{axis.ticks =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.title.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{    )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{rfm}{%
\section{RFM}\label{rfm}}

\href{https://cran.r-project.org/web/packages/rfm/vignettes/rfm-customer-level-data.html}{RFM} is calculated as:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  A recency score is assigned to each customer based on date of most recent purchase.
\item
  A frequency ranking is assigned based on frequency of purchases
\item
  Monetary score is assigned based on the total revenue generated by the customer in the period under consideration for the analysis
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rfm"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{rfm\_data\_customer}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 39,999 x 5
##    customer_id revenue most_recent_visit number_of_orders recency_days
##          <dbl>   <dbl> <date>                       <dbl>        <dbl>

##  2        2290    1555 2006-09-08                      16          115
##  3       26377     336 2006-11-19                       5           43
##  4       24650    1189 2006-10-29                      12           64
##  5       12883    1229 2006-12-09                      12           23
##  6        2119     929 2006-10-21                      11           72
##  7       31283    1569 2006-09-11                      17          112
##  8       33815     778 2006-08-12                      11          142
##  9       15972     641 2006-11-19                       9           43
## 10       27650     970 2006-08-23                      10          131
## # ... with 39,989 more rows
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# a unique customer id}
\CommentTok{\# number of transaction/order}
\CommentTok{\# total revenue from the customer}
\CommentTok{\# number of days since the last visit}


\NormalTok{rfm\_data\_orders }\CommentTok{\# to generate data\_orders, use rfm\_table\_order()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# unique customer id}
\CommentTok{\# date of transaction}
\CommentTok{\# and amount}
\CommentTok{\# customer\_id: name of the customer id column}
\CommentTok{\# order\_date: name of the transaction date column}
\CommentTok{\# revenue: name of the transaction amount column}
\CommentTok{\# analysis\_date: date of analysis}
\CommentTok{\# recency\_bins: number of rankings for recency score (default is 5)}
\CommentTok{\# frequency\_bins: number of rankings for frequency score (default is 5)}
\CommentTok{\# monetary\_bins: number of rankings for monetary score (default is 5)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{analysis\_date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ lubridate}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{as\_date}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2007{-}01{-}01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{rfm\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{rfm\_table\_customer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        rfm\_data\_customer,}
\NormalTok{        customer\_id,}
\NormalTok{        number\_of\_orders,}
\NormalTok{        recency\_days,}
\NormalTok{        revenue,}
\NormalTok{        analysis\_date}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{rfm\_result}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

##  2        2290          115                16   1555             4
##  3       26377           43                 5    336             5
##  4       24650           64                12   1189             5
##  5       12883           23                12   1229             5
##  6        2119           72                11    929             5

##  8       33815          142                11    778             3
##  9       15972           43                 9    641             5

## #   monetary_score <int>, rfm_score <dbl>

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# customer\_id: unique customer id}
\CommentTok{\# date\_most\_recent: date of most recent visit}
\CommentTok{\# recency\_days: days since the most recent visit}
\CommentTok{\# transaction\_count: number of transactions of the customer}
\CommentTok{\# amount: total revenue generated by the customer}
\CommentTok{\# recency\_score: recency score of the customer}
\CommentTok{\# frequency\_score: frequency score of the customer}
\CommentTok{\# monetary\_score: monetary score of the customer}
\CommentTok{\# rfm\_score: RFM score of the customer}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{visualization}{%
\subsection{Visualization}\label{visualization}}

\hypertarget{rfmc}{%
\subsection{RFMC}\label{rfmc}}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\Alph{enumi})}
\setcounter{enumi}{2}
\tightlist
\item
  clumpiness is defined as the degree of nonconformity to equal spacing \citep{Zhang_2015}
\end{enumerate}

In finance, clumpiness can indicate high growth potential but large risk, Hence, it can be incorporated into firm acquisition decision. Originated from sports phenomenon - hot hand effect - where success leads to more success.

In statistics, clumpiness is the serial dependence or ``non-constant propensity, specifically temporary elevations of propensity--- i.e.~periods during which one event is more likely to occur than the average level.'' \citep{Zhang_2013}

Properties of clumpiness:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Min (max) if events are equally spaced (close to one another)
\item
  Continuity
\item
  Convergence
\end{itemize}

\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{)}

                                             \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                                         ))}
\NormalTok{                              ))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.rec =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{                )}

\hypertarget{clv-cac}{%
\subsubsection{CLV \& CAC}\label{clv-cac}}

\href{https://www.analyzecore.com/2015/02/19/customer-segmentation-lifecycle-grids-clv-and-cac-with-r/}{calculate} customer acquisition cost (CAC) and customer lifetime value (CLV)

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# loading libraries}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggplot2)}

\CommentTok{\# creating data sample}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{product =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.65}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{order }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{),}
                    \AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{gender }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{),}
                     \AttributeTok{gender =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
                         \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}male\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}female\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                         \DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{,}
                         \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
                         \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.40}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.60}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                     ))}
\NormalTok{date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{),}
                   \AttributeTok{orderdate =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{((}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(data, order, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}orderId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, gender, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, date, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}orderId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders[orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, ]}
\NormalTok{orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate, }\AttributeTok{origin =} \StringTok{"2012{-}01{-}01"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# creating data frames with CAC and Gross margin}
\NormalTok{cac }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clientId),}
               \AttributeTok{cac =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{288}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{gr.margin }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{product =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
               \AttributeTok{grossmarg =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{))}

\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{(data, date, order, gender)}

\CommentTok{\# reporting date}
\NormalTok{today }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2012{-}04{-}11\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%Y{-}\%m{-}\%d\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# calculating customer lifetime value}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, gr.margin, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{clv }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{clv =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(grossmarg)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}

\CommentTok{\# processing data}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        orders,}
\NormalTok{        orderId }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ clientId }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ gender }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ orderdate }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ product,}
        \AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =}\NormalTok{ length}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{frequency =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
           \AttributeTok{recency =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(today }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ orderdate)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(orderdate }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderdate)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(orderId }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderId)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}

\NormalTok{orders.segm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.freq =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                              \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                  \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                  \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}3\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                         \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                             \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}4\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                             \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                                         ))}
\NormalTok{                              ))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.rec =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{),}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}0{-}6 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{7}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{),}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}7{-}13 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{19}\NormalTok{),}
                \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}14{-}19 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                    \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{),}
                    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}20{-}45 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{46}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{80}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}46{-}80 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}80 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                )}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# creating last cart feature}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cart =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(a }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(b }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(c }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{    )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(clientId)}

\CommentTok{\# defining order of boundaries}
\NormalTok{orders.segm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.freq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(orders.segm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.freq, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}4\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}3\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{orders.segm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.rec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        orders.segm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.rec,}
        \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}80 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}46{-}80 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}20{-}45 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}14{-}19 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}7{-}13 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}0{-}6 days\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{orders.segm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders.segm, cac, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders.segm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders.segm, clv, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{lcg.clv }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders.segm }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(segm.rec, segm.freq) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{quantity =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
              \CommentTok{\# calculating cumulative CAC and CLV}
              \AttributeTok{cac =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(cac),}
              \AttributeTok{clv =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(clv)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating CAC and CLV per client}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cac1 =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cac }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
           \AttributeTok{clv1 =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(clv }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{cohort-analysis}{%
\subsubsection{Cohort Analysis}\label{cohort-analysis}}

\href{https://www.analyzecore.com/2015/04/01/cohort-analysis-and-lifecycle-grids-mixed-segmentation-with-r/}{link}

combine customers through common characteristics to split customers into homogeneous groups

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# loading libraries}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggplot2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(googleVis)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

where

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  campaign, which includes campaign name and customer acquisition cost for each customer,
\item
  margin, which includes gross margin for each product,
\item
  potential, which includes potential values / predicted CLV for each client,
\item
  orders, which includes orders from our customers with products and order dates.
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# calculating CLV, frequency, recency, average time lapses between purchases and defining cohorts}

\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, gr.margin, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}product\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# combining products and summarising gross margin}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(orderId, clientId, orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{grossmarg =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(grossmarg)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating frequency, recency, average time lapses between purchases and defining cohorts}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{frequency =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{recency =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(today }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderdate)),}
        \AttributeTok{av.gap =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(orderdate)) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ frequency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{cohort =} \FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(orderdate), }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%Y{-}\%m\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating CLV to date}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId, cohort, frequency, recency, av.gap) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{clv =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(grossmarg)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
