\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# calculating potential CLV and CAC}
\NormalTok{customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(customers, campaign, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(customers, potential, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\# leading the potential value to more or less real value}
\NormalTok{customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clv.p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clv.p }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sqrt}\NormalTok{(customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{recency) }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{frequency,}
          \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{(potential, gr.margin, today)}
\CommentTok{\# adding segments}
\NormalTok{customers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ customers }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.freq =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                              \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                  \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                  \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}3\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                         \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                             \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}4\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                             \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                                         ))}
\NormalTok{                              ))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.rec =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{),}
        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}0{-}30 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{31}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}31{-}60 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{61}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{),}
                \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}61{-}90 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                    \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{91}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{120}\NormalTok{),}
                    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}91{-}120 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{121}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}121{-}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                )}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    ))}

\CommentTok{\# defining order of boundaries}
\NormalTok{customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.freq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.freq, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}4\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}3\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.rec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        customers}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{segm.rec,}
        \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}121{-}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}91{-}120 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}

            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}31{-}60 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{    )}

\CommentTok{\# 2. Analyzing customer flows}
\CommentTok{\# customers flows analysis (FPD cohorts)}

\CommentTok{\# defining cohort and reporting dates}
\NormalTok{coh }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2012{-}09\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{report.dates }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2012{-}10{-}01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2013{-}01{-}01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2013{-}04{-}01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# defining segments for each cohort\textquotesingle{}s customer for reporting dates}
\NormalTok{df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(report.dates)) \{}
\NormalTok{    orders.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

        \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{product,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{grossmarg) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{frequency =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
            \AttributeTok{recency =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(report.dates[i] }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderdate)),}
            \AttributeTok{cohort =} \FunctionTok{format}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(orderdate), }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\%Y{-}\%m\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{        ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

        \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(cohort }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ coh) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.freq =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
                \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}2 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                    \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
                    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}3 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                        \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{),}
                        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}4 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}5 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}5 purch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                    )}
\NormalTok{                )}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm.rec =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{),}
            \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}0{-}30 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{31}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
                \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}31{-}60 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                    \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{61}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{),}
                    \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}61{-}90 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                        \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{91}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{120}\NormalTok{),}
                        \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}91{-}120 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                        \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{121}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}121{-}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textgreater{}180 days\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                    )}
\NormalTok{                )}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
            \AttributeTok{cohort.segm =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(cohort, segm.rec, segm.freq, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{} : \textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{report.date =}\NormalTok{ report.dates[i]}
\NormalTok{        ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(clientId, cohort.segm, report.date)}
    
\NormalTok{    df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df.sankey, customers.cache)}
\NormalTok{\}}

    \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df.sankey,}
\NormalTok{          clientId }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ report.date,}

\FunctionTok{write.csv}\NormalTok{(df.sankey, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}customers\_path.csv\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{row.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.sankey }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{clientId)}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df.sankey)) \{}
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.sankey }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
        \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{} (\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, report.dates[i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
        \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{} (\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, report.dates[i], }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\hypertarget{campaign-cohorts}{%
\paragraph{Campaign Cohorts}\label{campaign-cohorts}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# campaign cohorts}
\NormalTok{lcg.camp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ customers }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(campaign, segm.rec, segm.freq) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating cumulative values}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{quantity =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{cac =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(cac),}
        \AttributeTok{clv =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(clv),}
        \AttributeTok{clv.p =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(clv.p),}
        \AttributeTok{av.gap =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(av.gap)}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating average values}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{av.cac =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cac }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{av.clv =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(clv }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{av.clv.p =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(clv.p }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{av.clv.tot =}\NormalTok{ av.clv }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ av.clv.p,}
        \AttributeTok{av.gap =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(av.gap }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ quantity, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{diff =}\NormalTok{ av.clv }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ av.cac}
\NormalTok{    )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# creating data sample}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{cohorts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{cohort =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cohort\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(}
            \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{36}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}d\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{flag =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}0\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{        ), }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\_\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{Y\_00 =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1300}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1500}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{36}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{Y\_01 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{800}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{36}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)),}
        \AttributeTok{Y\_02 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{600}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{800}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{24}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{)),}
        \AttributeTok{Y\_03 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{400}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{500}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{24}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{    )}
\CommentTok{\# simulating seasonality (Black Friday)}
\NormalTok{cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{*} \FloatTok{1.25}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{*} \FloatTok{1.10}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(cohorts[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{23}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{*} \FloatTok{1.07}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# calculating retention rate and preparing data for plotting}
\NormalTok{df\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
\NormalTok{    reshape2}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{melt}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        cohorts,}
        \AttributeTok{id.vars =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cohort\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{value.name =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}number\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{variable.name =} \StringTok{"year\_of\_LT"}
\NormalTok{    )}

\NormalTok{df\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_plot }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(cohort) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(year\_of\_LT) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{number\_prev\_year =} \FunctionTok{lag}\NormalTok{(number),}
           \AttributeTok{number\_Y\_00 =}\NormalTok{ number[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(year\_of\_LT }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Y\_00\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{ret\_rate\_prev\_year =}\NormalTok{ number }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ number\_prev\_year,}
        \AttributeTok{ret\_rate =}\NormalTok{ number }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ number\_Y\_00,}
        \AttributeTok{year\_cohort =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(year\_of\_LT, cohort, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}{-}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\# The first way for plotting cycle plot via scaling}
\CommentTok{\# calculating the coefficient for scaling 2nd axis}
\NormalTok{k }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{number\_prev\_year[df\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{year\_of\_LT }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Y\_01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{*} \FloatTok{1.15}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(df\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ret\_rate[df\_plot}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{year\_of\_LT }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}Y\_01\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{])}

\CommentTok{\# retention rate cycle plot}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(df\_plot),}
    \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ year\_cohort,}
        \AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ ret\_rate,}
        \AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ year\_of\_LT,}
        \AttributeTok{color =}\NormalTok{ year\_of\_LT}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{theme\_bw}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_text}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{label =} \FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(ret\_rate, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{))),}
        \AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{hjust =} \FloatTok{0.4}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{vjust =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{fontface =} \StringTok{"plain"}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \CommentTok{\# smooth method can be changed (e.g. for "lm")}

    \AttributeTok{M06 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{900}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{900}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1200}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1600}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1900}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{10300}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{M07 =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{850}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{900}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1300}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1300}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1900}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{13000}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}

\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{cohort.clients.r }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cohort.clients }\CommentTok{\#create new data frame}
\NormalTok{totcols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(cohort.clients.r) }\CommentTok{\#count number of columns in data set}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(cohort.clients.r)) \{}
    \CommentTok{\#for loop for shifting each row}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cohort.clients.r[i,] }\CommentTok{\#select row from data frame}
\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[, }\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{df[] }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{] }\CommentTok{\#remove columns with zeros}
\NormalTok{    partcols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
        \FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df) }\CommentTok{\#count number of columns in row (w/o zeros)}
    \CommentTok{\#fill columns after values by zeros}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (partcols }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ totcols)}
\NormalTok{        df[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{((partcols }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{totcols)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{    cohort.clients.r[i,] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\CommentTok{\#replace initial row by new one}
\NormalTok{\}}
\CommentTok{\# Retention ratio = \# clients in particular month / \# clients in 1st month of life{-}time}

\CommentTok{\#calculate retention (1)}
\NormalTok{x }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cohort.clients.r[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{)]}
\NormalTok{y }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cohort.clients.r[, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]}

\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ y)}
\NormalTok{reten.r }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cohort =}\NormalTok{ (cohort.clients.r}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cohort), reten.r)}

\CommentTok{\#calculate retention (2)}
\NormalTok{c }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(cohort.clients.r)}
\NormalTok{reten.r }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cohort.clients.r}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{c) \{}
\NormalTok{    reten.r[, (c }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ reten.r[, i] }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ reten.r[, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{\}}
\NormalTok{reten.r }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ reten.r[,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{c)]}
\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(reten.r) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(cohort.clients.r)}




\CommentTok{\#charts}
\NormalTok{reten.r }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ reten.r[,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\CommentTok{\#remove M01 data because it is always 100\%}
\CommentTok{\#dynamics analysis chart}
\NormalTok{cohort.chart1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{melt}\NormalTok{(reten.r, }\AttributeTok{id.vars =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cohort\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(cohort.chart1) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cohort\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}month\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}retention\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{cohort.chart1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(cohort.chart1, retention }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}

    \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(cohort.chart1,}
           \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{           ))}
\NormalTok{p }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\hypertarget{lifecycle-phase-sequential-analysis}{%
\subsubsection{Lifecycle phase sequential analysis}\label{lifecycle-phase-sequential-analysis}}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  analyze the path patterns of each cohort\\
\item
  identify cohorts that attracted customers with the path we prefer to make offers.
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(TraMineR)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{min.date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate)}
\NormalTok{max.date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate)}

\NormalTok{l }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{seq}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(max.date }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ min.date), }\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(max.date }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ min.date))}

\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ l) \{}
\NormalTok{    cur.date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ min.date }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ i}
    \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(cur.date)}
    
\NormalTok{    orders.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(orderdate }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}=}\NormalTok{ cur.date)}
    
\NormalTok{    customers.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders.cache }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{product,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{grossmarg) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{frequency =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{(),}
               \AttributeTok{recency =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(cur.date }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(orderdate))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(clientId, frequency, recency) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{segm =}
                   \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                       \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&} \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
                       \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}new customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                       \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                           \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}
                               \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{61}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{),}
                           \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}under risk new customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                           \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                               \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ recency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{,}
                               \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}1x buyer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                               
                               \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                   \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}
                                       \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
                                   \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}engaged customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                   \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                       \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}
                                           \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{61}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{),}
                                       \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}under risk engaged customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                       \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
                                           \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(frequency, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ recency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{,}
                                           \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}former engaged customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                           
                                           \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                                               frequency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\&} \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
                                               \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}best customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                               \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{                                                   frequency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\&}
                                                       \FunctionTok{between}\NormalTok{(recency, }\DecValTok{61}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{),}
                                                   \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}under risk best customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                                                   \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(frequency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\&}
\NormalTok{                                                              recency }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{180}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}former best customer\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                                               )}
\NormalTok{                                           )}
\NormalTok{                                       )}
\NormalTok{                                   )}
\NormalTok{                               )}
\NormalTok{                           )}
\NormalTok{                       )}
\NormalTok{                   )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{report.date =}\NormalTok{ i) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{    df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df, customers.cache)}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# converting data to the sequence format}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df,}
\NormalTok{          clientId }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ report.date,}
          \AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}segm\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df.seq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{seqdef}\NormalTok{(df,}
                 \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df),}
                 \AttributeTok{left =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}DEL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                 \AttributeTok{right =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}DEL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
                 \AttributeTok{xtstep =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# creating df with first purch.date and campaign cohort features}
\NormalTok{feat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(clientId)}
\NormalTok{feat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(feat, campaign[, }\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{feat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(feat, customers[, }\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{par}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mar =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# plotting the 10 most frequent sequences based on campaign}
\FunctionTok{seqfplot}\NormalTok{(df.seq, }\AttributeTok{border =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ feat}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{campaign)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\includegraphics{11-empirical_model_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-56-1.pdf}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# plotting the 10 most frequent sequences based on campaign}
\FunctionTok{seqfplot}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    df.seq,}
    \AttributeTok{border =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ feat}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{campaign,}
    \AttributeTok{cex.legend =} \FloatTok{0.9}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\includegraphics{11-empirical_model_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-56-2.pdf}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# plotting the 10 most frequent sequences based on first purch.date cohort}
\NormalTok{coh.list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(feat}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cohort))}
\CommentTok{\# defining cohorts for plotting}
\NormalTok{feat.coh.list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ feat[feat}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cohort }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ coh.list[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{] ,]}
\NormalTok{df.coh }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(clientId }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(feat.coh.list}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clientId))}
\NormalTok{df.seq.coh }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{seqdef}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        df.coh,}
        \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df.coh),}
        \AttributeTok{left =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}DEL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{right =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}DEL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{xtstep =} \DecValTok{10}
\NormalTok{    )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{shopping-carts-analysis}{%
\section{Shopping carts analysis}\label{shopping-carts-analysis}}

\hypertarget{multi-layer-pie-chart}{%
\subsection{Multi-layer pie chart}\label{multi-layer-pie-chart}}

Example by \href{https://www.analyzecore.com/2014/10/17/cart-analysis-r/}{Sergey Bryl}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# loading libraries}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(plotrix)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Attaching package: 'plotrix'
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## The following object is masked from 'package:scales':
## 
##     rescale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Simulate of orders}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{product =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}d\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.65}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,]}
\NormalTok{df}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# processing initial data}
\CommentTok{\# we need to be sure that product\textquotesingle{}s names are unique}
\NormalTok{df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#"}\NormalTok{, df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product, }\StringTok{"\#"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{prod.matrix }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# removing duplicated products from each order (exclude the effect of quantity)}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(orderId, product) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(product) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# combining products to cart and calculating number of products}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(orderId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cart =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(product, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{";"}\NormalTok{),}
              \AttributeTok{prod.num =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \CommentTok{\# calculating number of carts}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(cart, prod.num) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{num =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `summarise()` has grouped output by 'cart'. You can override using the `.groups` argument.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(prod.matrix)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

##   <chr>              <int> <int>

## 5 #a#;#b#;#d#            3   119
## 6 #a#;#c#                2    98
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# calculating total number of orders/carts}
\NormalTok{tot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(prod.matrix}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{num)}

\CommentTok{\# spliting orders for sets with 1 product and more than 1 product}
\NormalTok{one.prod }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ prod.matrix }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(prod.num }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{sev.prod }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ prod.matrix }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(prod.num }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(prod.num))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# defining parameters for pie chart}
\NormalTok{iniR }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FloatTok{0.2} \CommentTok{\# initial radius}
\NormalTok{cols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"\#ffffff"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fec44f"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#a1d99b"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fee0d2"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#2ca25f"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#8856a7"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#43a2ca"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#fdbb84"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#e34a33"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#a6bddb"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#dd1c77"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#ffeda0"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"\#756bb1"}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{prod }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(product) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(product) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{prod }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NO\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product))}
\NormalTok{colors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.list}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(cols[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(prod)))], prod))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 0 circle: blank}
\FunctionTok{pie}\NormalTok{(}
    \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{radius =}\NormalTok{ iniR,}
    \AttributeTok{init.angle =} \DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{col =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{border =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{labels =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# drawing circles from last to 2nd}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(prod)}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{(prod[i], sev.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart)}
\NormalTok{    col }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NO\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{times =} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(sev.prod))}
\NormalTok{    col[p] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ prod[i]}
    \FunctionTok{floating.pie}\NormalTok{(}
        \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
        \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(sev.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{num, tot }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(sev.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{num)),}
        \AttributeTok{radius =}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ i) }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ iniR,}
        \AttributeTok{startpos =}\NormalTok{ pi }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{col =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(colors [}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(col, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NO\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)]),}
        \AttributeTok{border =} \StringTok{"\#44aaff"}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# 1 circle: orders with 1 product}
\FunctionTok{floating.pie}\NormalTok{(}
    \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(tot }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(one.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{num), one.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{num),}
    \AttributeTok{radius =} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ iniR,}
    \AttributeTok{startpos =}\NormalTok{ pi }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{col =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(colors [}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NO\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, one.prod}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart)]),}
    \AttributeTok{border =} \StringTok{"\#44aaff"}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# legend}
\FunctionTok{legend}\NormalTok{(}
    \FloatTok{1.5}\NormalTok{,}
    \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ iniR,}
    \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{" "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(colors)[}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]),}
    \AttributeTok{col =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(colors [}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]),}
    \AttributeTok{pch =} \DecValTok{19}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{bty =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}n\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{ncol =} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# creating a table with the stats}
\NormalTok{stat.tab }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ prod.matrix }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{prod.num) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{share =}\NormalTok{ num }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ tot) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(num))}

\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(scales)}
\NormalTok{stat.tab}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{share }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{percent}\NormalTok{(stat.tab}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{share) }\CommentTok{\# converting values to percents}

\hypertarget{sankey-diagram}{%
\subsection{Sankey Diagram}\label{sankey-diagram}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# loading libraries}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(googleVis)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}

\CommentTok{\# creating an example of orders}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{prod1 =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{prod2 =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{prod3 =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# combining products}
\NormalTok{df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{prod1, df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{prod2, df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{prod3, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{};\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL;|;NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart)}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df[df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cart }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,]}

\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(orderId, clientId, cart) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(clientId, orderId, cart)}

\FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(df)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##   orderId clientId  cart
## 1     181        1     b
## 2     282        1     a
## 3     748        1 a;b;c
## 4      27        2   a;b
## 5     209        2   b;c
## 6     244        2 a;b;c
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n.ord =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ord\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()), }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}

\FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(orders)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 6 x 4
##   orderId clientId cart  n.ord
##     <int>    <int> <chr> <chr>
## 1     181        1 b     ord1 
## 2     282        1 a     ord2 
## 3     748        1 a;b;c ord3 
## 4      27        2 a;b   ord1 
## 5     209        2 b;c   ord2 
## 6     244        2 a;b;c ord3
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(orders,}
\NormalTok{          clientId }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ n.ord,}
          \AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cart\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}


\CommentTok{\# choose a number of carts/orders in the sequence we want to analyze}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(ord1, ord2, ord3, ord4, ord5)}

\NormalTok{orders.plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(orders)) \{}
\NormalTok{    ord.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(orders[, i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], orders[, i]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
    
    \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(ord.cache)[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}from\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}to\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
    
    \CommentTok{\# adding tags to carts}
\NormalTok{    ord.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(ord.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ord.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(ord.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, i, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    orders.plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(orders.plot, ord.cache)}
    
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{sequence-in-depth-analysis}{%
\subsection{Sequence in-depth analysis}\label{sequence-in-depth-analysis}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(TraMineR)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(reshape2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(googleVis)}

\CommentTok{\# creating an example of shopping carts}
\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{product =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}a\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}b\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}c\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
        \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.65}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{order }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{),}
                    \AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{sex }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{clientId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{),}
                  \AttributeTok{sex =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{(}
                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}male\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}female\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{),}
                      \DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{,}
                      \AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
                      \AttributeTok{prob =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.40}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.60}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                  ))}
\NormalTok{date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{orderId =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{),}
                   \AttributeTok{orderdate =} \FunctionTok{sample}\NormalTok{((}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{1000}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{replace =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(data, order, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}orderId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, sex, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}clientId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(orders, date, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}orderId\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{orders }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders[orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{product }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}NULL\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,]}
\NormalTok{orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(orders}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate, }\AttributeTok{origin =} \StringTok{"2012{-}01{-}01"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{(data, date, order, sex)}

\CommentTok{\# combining products to the cart}
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ orders }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(product) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{orderId) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(clientId, sex, orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cart =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(product, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{";"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{max.date }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{ids }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clientId)}
\NormalTok{df.new }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(ids)) \{}
\NormalTok{    df.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(clientId }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ ids[i])}
    
    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{           av.dur }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{           av.dur }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
               \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(((}
                   \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \FunctionTok{min}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate)}
\NormalTok{               ) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ (}
                   \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache) }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{               )) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \FloatTok{1.5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}
    
\NormalTok{    df.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
        \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            df.cache,}
            \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
                \AttributeTok{clientId =}\NormalTok{ df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{clientId[}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache)],}
                \AttributeTok{sex =}\NormalTok{ df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sex[}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache)],}
                \AttributeTok{orderdate =} \FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ av.dur,}
                \AttributeTok{cart =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{            )}
\NormalTok{        )}
    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{max}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ max.date,}
\NormalTok{           df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate[}\FunctionTok{which.max}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ max.date,}
           \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(df.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{orderdate[}\DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache)] }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, max.date)}
    
    \CommentTok{\# order\# for Sankey diagram}
\NormalTok{    df.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.cache }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ord =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}ord\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df.cache)), }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{))}
    
\NormalTok{    df.new }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df.new, df.cache)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\CommentTok{\# filtering dummies}
\NormalTok{df.new }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.new }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(cart }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ to }\SpecialCharTok{!=}\NormalTok{ orderdate)}
\FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{(orders, df, df.cache, i, ids, max.date, av.dur)}

\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#\# Sankey diagram \#\#\#\#\#\#\#}

\NormalTok{df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.new }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(clientId, cart, ord)}

\NormalTok{df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{dcast}\NormalTok{(df.sankey,}
\NormalTok{          clientId }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ ord,}
          \AttributeTok{value.var =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}cart\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{fun.aggregate =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{df.sankey[}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(df.sankey)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}unknown\textquotesingle{}}

\CommentTok{\# chosing a length of sequence}
\NormalTok{df.sankey }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.sankey }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(ord1, ord2, ord3, ord4)}

\CommentTok{\# replacing NAs after \textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{} for \textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{df.sankey[df.sankey[, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{df.sankey[df.sankey[, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}nopurch\textquotesingle{}}

\NormalTok{df.sankey.plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{ncol}\NormalTok{(df.sankey)) \{}
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df.sankey }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(df.sankey[, i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], df.sankey[, i]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
        \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
    
    \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.cache)[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}from\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}to\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
    
    \CommentTok{\# adding tags to carts}
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
        \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{from, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.cache}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{to, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}(\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, i, }\StringTok{\textquotesingle{})\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    df.sankey.plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rbind}\NormalTok{(df.sankey.plot, df.sankey.cache)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\hypertarget{qualitative-research}{%
\chapter{Qualitative Research}\label{qualitative-research}}

\hypertarget{inter-rate-reliability-methods}{%
\section{Inter-rate reliability methods}\label{inter-rate-reliability-methods}}

Calculation to judge the degree of agreement between the choices made by two or more independent judges

Other packages are

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \texttt{vcd} for visualization
\item
  \texttt{DescTools}
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{percent-agreement}{%
\subsection{Percent Agreement}\label{percent-agreement}}

\[
\frac{\text{number of agreement}}{\text{number of total}} \times 100
\]

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"irr"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Loading required package: lpSolve
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"diagnoses"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{package =} \StringTok{"irr"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"anxiety"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{package =} \StringTok{"irr"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(diagnoses,}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##                     rater1                  rater2                  rater3
## 1              4. Neurosis             4. Neurosis             4. Neurosis
## 2  2. Personality Disorder 2. Personality Disorder 2. Personality Disorder
## 3  2. Personality Disorder        3. Schizophrenia        3. Schizophrenia
## 4                 5. Other                5. Other                5. Other
## 5  2. Personality Disorder 2. Personality Disorder 2. Personality Disorder
## 6            1. Depression           1. Depression        3. Schizophrenia
## 7         3. Schizophrenia        3. Schizophrenia        3. Schizophrenia

## 9            1. Depression           1. Depression             4. Neurosis
## 10                5. Other                5. Other                5. Other
##              rater4           rater5           rater6
## 1       4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis
## 2          5. Other         5. Other         5. Other
## 3  3. Schizophrenia 3. Schizophrenia         5. Other
## 4          5. Other         5. Other         5. Other
## 5       4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis
## 6  3. Schizophrenia 3. Schizophrenia 3. Schizophrenia
## 7  3. Schizophrenia         5. Other         5. Other
## 8  3. Schizophrenia 3. Schizophrenia      4. Neurosis
## 9       4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis      4. Neurosis
## 10         5. Other         5. Other         5. Other
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{agree}\NormalTok{(diagnoses)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\hypertarget{cohens-kappa}{%
\subsection{Cohen's Kappa}\label{cohens-kappa}}

\citep{Cohen_1960}

\[
k = \frac{p_o - p_e}{1 - p_e} = 1 - \frac{1 - p_o}{1- p_e}
\]

where

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \(p_o\) = relative observed agreement among raters
\item
  \(p_e\) = hypothetical probability of chance agreement
\end{itemize}

strict agreements between raters

appropriate in case of 2 ordinal or nominal varibles

Based on \citep{Landis_1977}'s guide, we have

\begin{longtable}[]{@{}ll@{}}
\toprule
Degree & Decision\tabularnewline
\midrule
\endhead
0.01 -- 0.20 & slight agreement\tabularnewline
0.21 -- 0.40 & fair agreement\tabularnewline
0.41 -- 0.60 & moderate agreement\tabularnewline
0.61 -- 0.80 & substantial agreement\tabularnewline
0.81 -- 1.00 & almost perfect or perfect agreement\tabularnewline
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Unweighted kappa for 2 nominal or 2 ordinal categorical}
\FunctionTok{kappa2}\NormalTok{(diagnoses[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rater1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"rater2"}\NormalTok{)], }\AttributeTok{weight =} \StringTok{"unweighted"}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# two ordinal variables only, allows partial agreement}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##  Cohen's Kappa for 2 Raters (Weights: unweighted)
## 
##  Subjects = 30 
##    Raters = 2 
##     Kappa = 0.651 
## 
##         z = 7 
##   p-value = 2.63e-12
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Weighted kappa ordinal scales}
\FunctionTok{kappa2}\NormalTok{(diagnoses[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rater1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"rater2"}\NormalTok{)], }\AttributeTok{weight =} \StringTok{"equal"}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# linear weightes of the differences}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##  Cohen's Kappa for 2 Raters (Weights: equal)
## 
##  Subjects = 30 
##    Raters = 2 
##     Kappa = 0.633 
## 
##         z = 5.43 
##   p-value = 5.52e-08
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{kappa2}\NormalTok{(diagnoses[, }\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rater1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"rater2"}\NormalTok{)], }\AttributeTok{weight =} \StringTok{"squared"}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# squared weightes of the differences}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##  Cohen's Kappa for 2 Raters (Weights: squared)
## 
##  Subjects = 30 
##    Raters = 2 
##     Kappa = 0.655 
## 
##         z = 3.91 
##   p-value = 9.37e-05
\end{verbatim}

p-value less than 0.05, mean that raters agree more than what you would expect by chance.

\hypertarget{fleisskappa}{%
\subsection{Fleiss'kappa}\label{fleisskappa}}

for two or more categorical variables (nominal or ordinal)

for three or more raters

\begin{itemize}
\item
  0 = no agreement
\item
  1 = perfect agreement
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# no assumption of same raters for all subjects}
\FunctionTok{kappam.fleiss}\NormalTok{(diagnoses[, }\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{])}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##  Fleiss' Kappa for m Raters
## 
##  Subjects = 30 
##    Raters = 3 
##     Kappa = 0.534 
## 
##         z = 9.89 
##   p-value = 0
\end{verbatim}

\hypertarget{krippendorffs-alpha}{%
\section{Krippendorff's Alpha}\label{krippendorffs-alpha}}

calculates disagreement among raters.

\[
\frac{\text{observed disagreement}}{\text{expected disagreement}}
\]

Rules of thumb by \citep{Shelley_1984}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \(\alpha \ge 0.08\) = good
\item
  \(\alpha \ge 0.667\) = acceptable lower limit
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{nmm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{matrix}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
            \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
            \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
            \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}

Continuous ordinal ratings

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{rtr1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{rtr3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{ratings }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{cbind}\NormalTok{(rtr1, rtr2, rtr3)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(DescTools)}
\end{Highlighting}

\hypertarget{intraclass-correlation-coefficients}{%
\subsection{Intraclass correlation coefficients}\label{intraclass-correlation-coefficients}}

\citep{Shrout_1979}

\hypertarget{continuous-scales}{%
\subsubsection{Continuous scales}\label{continuous-scales}}

Decision:

\begin{itemize}
\item
  model:

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \texttt{"oneway"} when subjects are random effects
  \item
    \texttt{"twoway"} when subjects and raters are random effects
  \end{itemize}
\item
  type:

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \texttt{"agreement"} differences in mean ratings among raters are of interest
  \item
    \texttt{"consistency"} is default
  \end{itemize}
\item
  unit

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    \texttt{"single"} single value
  \item
    \texttt{"average"} mean of several ratings
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{icc}\NormalTok{(anxiety,}
    \AttributeTok{model =} \StringTok{"twoway"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# can be "oneway"}
    \AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"agreement"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# can be "consistency"}
    \AttributeTok{unit =} \StringTok{"single"} \CommentTok{\# can be "average"}
\NormalTok{    )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##  Single Score Intraclass Correlation
## 
##    Model: twoway 
##    Type : agreement 
## 
##    Subjects = 20 
##      Raters = 3 
##    ICC(A,1) = 0.198
## 
##  F-Test, H0: r0 = 0 ; H1: r0 > 0 
##  F(19,39.7) = 1.83 , p = 0.0543 
## 
##  95%-Confidence Interval for ICC Population Values:
##   -0.039 < ICC < 0.494
\end{verbatim}

\texttt{DescTools} package

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{rtr1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{9}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{7}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{rtr2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,  }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{rtr3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,  }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{rtr4 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,  }\DecValTok{9}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{7}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{ratings }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{cbind}\NormalTok{(rtr1, rtr2, rtr3, rtr4)}
\NormalTok{DescTools}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{ICC}\NormalTok{(ratings)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

\hypertarget{nudges}{%
\chapter{Nudges}\label{nudges}}

\citep{Garbinsky_2020} uncovers that people under save because they have positive illusion of financial responsibility (i.e.,
they thought they are financially more responsible than the average).

\citep{Mrkva_2021} found that choice architecture influences inequities across people. More specifically, people with the
following dimensions enjoy the most benefit from nudges:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Low socioeconomic status (SES)
\item
  Low numerical ability
\item
  Low knowledge
\end{itemize}

Hence, we should use nudges to reduce SES disparities.
